# A static inward multiscale staircase on the original small-tau ruler

Project: gd-exact-profile-20260926. Worker: max3. Allocated round: 59.
Controlling guidance: c2181daa6b96056c. Frozen policy: 117f41cdec34f009.

**Status: complete JOINT UNVERIFIED proof candidate. No fact_id.**
The assignment permits one analytical round and forbids formal submissions.
Every new calculation below is part of this single joint proof attempt;
none is an accepted intermediate result. The original exact minimax problem
remains OPEN. No computation, trajectory evaluation, recurrence execution,
solver, mathematical replay, consult, or subagent was used.

The candidate is one one-dimensional static function with m-1 distinct
positive gradient magnitudes and m-1 actual ordinary-block shelf visits.
Its derivative is continuous and piecewise affine, with slopes only zero
and one. The number of spatial layers grows with m. All original steps
are retained, the minimum is attained uniquely at zero, and the closest
initial distance is calculated exactly.

This chosen mechanism fails to improve the same-word Huber payoff. For
every integer m>=8, its actual normalized terminal payoff V_m satisfies

    0 < V_m/H_base < 2*(25/49)^(m-3) < 1,
    H_base = 1/(1+2*S_m).                                      (A)

More generally, the same bound holds for the precise inward, disjoint,
constant-gradient block class defined in Section 8. That statement allows
arbitrary legal connectors and inner function values. It is NOT a bound
on all one-dimensional functions, overlapping returns, trajectories with
varying gradients inside those blocks, or the unrestricted W_n.

The exact constructed payoff is

    V_m = (1+2*S_(m-1))/A_0^2,                               (B)

where A_0 is the explicit backward-defined starting radius below. Formula
(B), the entire integrated function, and the bound (A) are all established
jointly. The proof does not end with an uncontrolled switching recurrence.

## 1. Recalled scopes and source discipline

The following shared drafts are awareness, not proof premises.

* 69b90f738a77a91b excludes all squared distances to an interval on its
  original tau=1 word, and one specified inward-return initialization of
  a fixed one-sided Huber function. It explicitly exhibits another start
  of that same function with Huber-scale payoff. Its fixed branch is not
  being retuned here.
* 1a23bf9f1a7799fd concerns a common-ell log-periodic staircase on the
  different c_sc=13/5, a_sc=c_sc^(-17), no-plus-one word. Independent ray
  phases do not remove its common-ell restriction.
* 3d2994746c1d983e allows arbitrary profiles on that different word only
  when all nonzero queried secants on each ray fit a factor-13/5 band.
* e08ee0674ce368d3 concerns the original tau=1 word but imposes a common
  reciprocal/small-curvature core, subpower derivative growth, and stated
  ray-comparison bounds. The outer unit ramps constructed below are not
  subpower ramps: on such a ramp r*Q'(r)/Q(r)>1.

Accepted fact 1fca3e5dd555cb4a constructs outward alternating-ray shelf
extensions and pays retained offsets and areas. Its extension rule is
not the inward nesting rule here, so its recurrence is not imported.
Accepted fact 1252b8823bb5a290 fixes the full directed interpolation and
W=2R conventions. We prove those inequalities directly for the function.
Accepted fact 04219554e41d9750 supplies only the unconditional ORIGINAL
benchmark definitions and positive finite ray limit used in Section 9.
No conditional seed-existence assertion is assumed.

Matlas was queried for the multiscale scalar construction mechanism. Its
2303.03534 small-step convergence result and 2306.07098 graph-learning
results were inapplicable. A primary-source web search located Altschuler
and Parrilo, *Acceleration by Stepsize Hedging II: Silver Stepsize Schedule
for Smooth Convex Optimization*, arXiv:2309.16530v1 (2023). Its exact HTML
and extracted text were saved in downloads/; Section 2, the opening of
Section 3, and Section 3.2 were read. The schedule and normalization differ
from ours, and its Silver gluing certificate is not applied to the perturbed
word. This was background on physical indexing and full pair consistency,
not an external theorem premise. All needed scalar arguments follow below.

## 2. The unchanged physical word and elementary inequalities

Fix an integer m>=8 and use exactly

    tau=1/4, lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta_word=1-1/(4*m),
    c=lambda*(1+1/(4*m)), a=beta_word/c,
    d_j=a*c^j,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), 0<=j<m,
    S_j=sum H_j, n=2^m-1.

The notation d_j means a bridge EXCESS, so the physical step is 1+d_j.
Every occurrence of H_j retains this SAME enclosing m and tau.
The recurrence for the actual mass is

    S_0=0, S_(j+1)=2*S_j+1+d_j.                           (2.1)

Useful strict bounds are

    2<c<5/2, 0<a<1/2, S_j>d_j for 1<=j<=m.             (2.2)

For c<5/2, note that sqrt(2)<47/33, since 2*33^2<47^2.
Thus lambda<80/33, and c<=33*lambda/32<5/2. The other
parameter bounds follow from lambda>2 and beta_word<1.
At j=1, S_1=1+a>d_1=beta_word. If S_j>d_j then

    S_(j+1)-d_(j+1)
      =2*(S_j-d_j)+1+(3-c)*d_j >0,

which proves the last inequality in (2.2).

Select precisely the first occurrences of levels 2,3,...,m-1. Put

    k=m-2,
    b_i=1+d_(i+2), 0<=i<k,
    s_0=S_2,
    s_i=S_(i+1), 1<=i<=k.

The word, without omissions, is then

    (H_2,b_0,H_2,b_1,H_3,b_2,...,H_(m-2),b_(k-1),H_(m-1)). (2.3)

This follows by successively expanding the LEFT child of H_m. The
selected checkpoint b_i is at physical zero-based index 2^(i+2)-1.
Stage 0 begins at index zero; stage i>=1 begins at index 2^(i+1).
The first block has length 3, each subsequent block i has length
2^(i+1)-1, and the complete horizon is exactly 2^m-1.

For 0<=i<=k-2 define D_i=b_i-1=d_(i+2); also set
D_(k-1)=d_(m-1). Then

    D_(i+1)=c*D_i, s_(i+1)=S_(i+2)>D_i.                 (2.4)

Write s=s_k=S_(m-1), D=D_(k-1)=d_(m-1), and T=1+2*s.
By (2.1)--(2.2),

    S_m=2*s+1+D, D<s,
    1+2*S_m < 3*(1+2*s)=3*T,
    1+S_2=4+2*a+beta_word>4.                            (2.5)

## 3. The frozen backward knot policy

All scalars in this section are defined before GD is run. They are exact
finite mathematical recurrences, not numerically evaluated recurrences.

Set

    q_k=1, A_k=T,
    q_(k-1)=A_k/D_(k-1),
    A_(k-1)=(1+s_(k-1))*q_(k-1).                        (3.1)

For i=k-2,k-3,...,0, define

    q_i=(A_(i+1)+A_(i+2)-q_(i+2))/D_i,
    A_i=(1+s_i)*q_i+A_(i+2)-q_(i+2).                    (3.2)

The symbols q_i and A_i will be the gradient magnitude and starting
radius of stage i. Put

    R_i=A_i-s_i*q_i, 0<=i<=k.                           (3.3)

We prove that these definitions give ordered legal knots, rather than
assuming that they describe a realizable switching sequence.

First, for every nonterminal stage the definitions imply

    A_i+A_(i+1)=(b_i+s_i)*q_i.                          (3.4)

For i=k-1 this is (3.1) because A_k=D_(k-1)*q_(k-1).
For earlier i, substitute (3.2) and D_i=b_i-1.

Second, backward induction gives

    A_i>=q_i>0 for every i,
    q_(k-1)>q_k,
    q_i>(c+1)*q_(i+1) for 0<=i<=k-2.                   (3.5)

The last base ratio is q_(k-1)=T/D>1 because s>D.
The two last A_i are at least their q_i. If the later claims hold,
use (3.4) at i+1 in the first line of (3.2) to obtain

    D_i*q_i=(b_(i+1)+s_(i+1))*q_(i+1)-q_(i+2)
            >(D_(i+1)+s_(i+1))*q_(i+1)
            >(c+1)*D_i*q_(i+1).                        (3.6)

The first strict inequality uses q_(i+1)>q_(i+2); the second uses
(2.4). Also A_(i+2)-q_(i+2)>=0, so (3.2) gives A_i>=q_i.
This proves all statements by backward induction.

For the innermost two stages,

    R_(k-1)=q_(k-1), R_k=1+s>q_k.

For every i<=k-2, (3.2)--(3.3) give

    R_i=A_(i+2)+q_i-q_(i+2)>A_(i+2),
    A_i>R_i>=q_i.                                      (3.7)

Thus, on each ray, every new inner shelf lies strictly inside the inner
endpoint of the previous outer shelf. Each connector has exactly its
gradient change as its length. This is the shortest connector permitted
by 1-Lipschitz continuity for these particular endpoint data; it is not
asserted to optimize the entire class of scalar functions.

## 4. One globally defined static gradient

Stage i uses ray sign (-1)^i. For each parity sigma in {0,1}, let ell_sigma
be the largest i<=k with that parity. Thus the two innermost indices are
k and k-1. Construct a radial derivative Q_sigma on r>=0 as follows.

For the innermost index ell=ell_sigma set

    Q_sigma(r)=r                  on 0<=r<=q_ell,
    Q_sigma(r)=q_ell              on q_ell<=r<=A_ell.

For i=ell-2,ell-4,...,sigma extend it outward by

    Q_sigma(r)=q_(i+2)+(r-A_(i+2))
                          on A_(i+2)<=r<=R_i,
    Q_sigma(r)=q_i         on R_i<=r<=A_i.               (4.1)

Beyond the outermost A_sigma keep Q_sigma equal to q_sigma. Equations
(3.5)--(3.7) prove that all intervals are correctly ordered, nonempty,
cover the ray with no gap, and agree at their endpoints. Every ramp in
(4.1) has slope one, and every shelf has slope zero. There are m-1
distinct positive shelf magnitudes in total, and m-1 nonempty ramps.

Define the single derivative and its integrated function on all of R by

    g(x)=Q_0(x) if x>=0, and g(x)=-Q_1(-x) if x<=0,
    F(x)=integral_0^x g(t) dt.                           (4.2)

This g is continuous, nondecreasing and 1-Lipschitz, since its ordered
affine pieces have slopes in {0,1}. At zero both formulas agree. It
follows directly that F is convex, differentiable, and 1-smooth with
F'=g. The gradient has slope one on a neighborhood of zero, so its
optimal global Lipschitz constant is exactly one.

Each Q_sigma is strictly positive for r>0. Consequently the ENTIRE
minimum set is {0}, and F(0)=min F=0. The prescribed initial point
x_0=A_0 has exact closest distance A_0 and squared distance A_0^2.
For any advertised L>0, use f=L*F with physical steps h_t/L. The
positions are identical and the normalized payoff is unchanged.

## 5. Integrated values, including every retained inner area

For each shelf define its ramp endpoint U_i by

    U_k=q_k=1,
    U_(k-1)=q_(k-1),
    U_i=R_i for i<=k-2.

Put l_i=A_i-U_i and define the retained area

    E_in_i=sum_(j>i, j<=k, j congruent i mod 2) q_j*l_j.  (5.1)

All sums are finite. The innermost E_in_k and E_in_(k-1) are zero.
For i<k, l_i=s_i*q_i; the final shelf instead has l_k=2*s.
The full radial value on ANY shelf is

    F((-1)^i*r)=q_i^2/2+E_in_i+q_i*(r-U_i),
                         U_i<=r<=A_i.                  (5.2)

For an outer ramp in (4.1), its full value is

    F((-1)^i*r)=F((-1)^i*A_(i+2))
       +q_(i+2)*(r-A_(i+2))+(r-A_(i+2))^2/2.            (5.3)

To verify these formulas, start with F=r^2/2 in each unit core. An
inner shelf contributes precisely q_j*l_j. Integrating the next unit
ramp contributes (q_i^2-q_(i+2)^2)/2, so the earlier shelf areas
remain as E_in_i. This proves (5.1)--(5.3) successively on each ray.
The outer constant continuation has the matching linear continuation
of (5.2). Therefore every value comes from the SAME integral (4.2).

At a later inner stage, the outer areas are not erased. They remain
part of F at outer points and cease contributing to F at smaller radii
because those outer intervals lie outside the integration segment
from zero. This distinction is the geometric reason inward movement
can remove terminal area while still paying its initial-distance cost.

There is also an explicit check against an illegal value reset. For
any i<=k-2, E_in_i>0. At the two actual queries R_i and A_i the gradient
is q_i. Their correct values differ by q_i*(A_i-R_i). Reset only the
value at R_i to q_i^2/2 while retaining the value at A_i. The directed
interpolation residual from A_i to R_i becomes exactly E_in_i>0.
Thus resetting a new core is not an alternative reading of this law.

## 6. Every physical GD update, all crossings, and spatial order

Let P_i(r) denote the sum of the first r physical steps of block i in
(2.3), including P_i(0)=0. Every value lies in [0,s_i], with endpoints
zero and s_i. At every query in
that block, and at its endpoint before a checkpoint or termination,
the actual iterate is

    x=(-1)^i*(A_i-q_i*P_i(r)).                           (6.1)

For i<k the bracket lies in [R_i,A_i], and for i=k it lies in
[1+s,T], a subset of [U_k,A_k]. Hence the actual gradient there is
(-1)^i*q_i. Every individual step h in the block therefore changes
(6.1) by exactly -h*g(x). This verifies all its steps, including its
unselected large bridges. Replacing a block by its sum was used only
after proving that EVERY partial sum stays in the same actual shelf.

At selected checkpoint i the incoming point is (-1)^i*R_i. Identity
(3.4) gives R_i=b_i*q_i-A_(i+1), so the actual update is

    (-1)^i*R_i-b_i*(-1)^i*q_i
       =(-1)^(i+1)*A_(i+1).                            (6.2)

This proves each full crossing. The derivative at the departure is
fixed by the old shelf; the landing point is on the already defined
opposite-ray shelf. Every intermediate spatial knot crossed by the
jump belongs to (4.1)--(4.2). A GD jump uses the gradient at departure,
and does not integrate or change gradients along the jump. No new
objective, knot, offset, or step is inserted at a crossing.

Starting from x_0=A_0, (6.1)--(6.2) prove the entire original H_m
trajectory by induction through its actual blocks. The terminal point is

    x_n=(-1)^k*(1+s).                                   (6.3)

The complete order of sampled radii is also fixed. In each block they
decrease strictly because every physical step is positive. On the same
ray, all samples of stage i are outside every sample of stage i+2,
since R_i>A_(i+2). Opposite rays are separated by zero. There are no
coincident query points or zero landings in this construction. Were the
same point queried twice, (4.2) would of course fix the same gradient
and value; the proof makes no freedom out of repeated queries.

## 7. All ordered interpolation rows and actual Gram positivity

Here is a direct proof for all real points, not just adjacent samples.
For u<v put delta=g(v)-g(u)>=0. Lipschitz continuity gives
delta<=v-u. Monotonicity and the same Lipschitz bound imply

    g(t)-g(u)>=max(0,delta-(v-t)),
    g(v)-g(t)>=max(0,delta-(t-u)),  u<=t<=v.

Integrate both inequalities over [u,v] to get

    F(v)-F(u)-g(u)*(v-u)>=delta^2/2,
    F(u)-F(v)-g(v)*(u-v)>=delta^2/2.                    (7.1)

Thus, for every ordered pair of actual queries, the terminal point,
or the actual optimum, in the project's convention,

    Q_ij=F(x_i)-F(x_j)+g(x_i)*(x_j-x_i)
                           +(g(x_i)-g(x_j))^2/2 <=0.   (7.2)

Equal points give equality. All remote rows and both orientations
follow from (7.1). For f=L*F the corresponding term is divided by L
in the usual way. The gradients and positions are actual real numbers,
so every required Gram matrix is an outer-product Gram matrix and is
positive semidefinite. This is not a numerically perturbed PSD claim.

## 8. Exact payoff and a full scoped inward-shelf obstruction

The terminal point (6.3) is on the innermost shelf of its ray. By the
integrated formula (5.2),

    F(x_n)=s+1/2=T/2,
    V_m=2*F(x_n)/A_0^2=T/A_0^2>0.                     (8.1)

We now prove the bound for a wider, explicitly delimited class. This
is part of the same joint proof, not an invocation of an unverified lemma.

Consider ANY single static convex differentiable 1-smooth scalar F
with an attained minimum, translated and reflected so that zero is
the closest minimizer to its positive initial point A_0. Use exactly
the blocks and selected checkpoints in (2.3). Suppose there are
positive A_i,q_i such that block i lies on the constant-gradient
shelf [R_i,A_i] on ray (-1)^i, including the terminal point, where
R_i=A_i-s_i*q_i>0. Suppose every selected checkpoint lands on the
next opposite-ray shelf start, and require precisely the inward order

    A_(i+2)<=R_i for 0<=i<=k-2.                         (8.2)

No connector is prescribed, and the gradient may be arbitrary outside
those shelves. No adjacent-gradient comparison is assumed. A minimum
interval extending to the left of zero is allowed; zero must remain
the closest minimizer to the positive initial point, so the denominator
is still A_0^2.

Static monotonicity and 1-Lipschitz continuity on each ray give

    q_i>=q_(i+2),
    R_i-A_(i+2)>=q_i-q_(i+2).                           (8.3)

The actual checkpoint identity is still (3.4). Combining it with
(8.3), rather than treating gradients as free controls, yields

    D_i*q_i >= A_(i+1)+A_(i+2)-q_(i+2)
             =(b_(i+1)+s_(i+1))*q_(i+1)-q_(i+2).        (8.4)

At any sampled radius r, the magnitude of the gradient is at most r,
because g(0)=0 and g is 1-Lipschitz. In particular R_i>=q_i.
Put z=A_k/q_k. Then R_k>=q_k gives z>=s+1. Also the last
checkpoint and R_(k-1)>=q_(k-1) give

    q_(k-1)>=A_k/D = z*q_k/D > q_k,                    (8.5)

where z>=s+1>D. Backward induction in (8.4), using (2.4), now proves

    q_i>(c+1)*q_(i+1) for every i<=k-2.                 (8.6)

Indeed, once q_(i+1)>q_(i+2), its right side is strictly greater
than (D_(i+1)+s_(i+1))*q_(i+1)>(c+1)*D_i*q_(i+1).
Thus the adjacent decrease is a CONSEQUENCE of full same-ray history.
It was not assumed or supplied by a freely switching curvature model.

Since A_0>= (1+s_0)*q_0, (8.5)--(8.6) imply

    A_0 > (1+S_2)*(c+1)^(k-1)*z*q_k/D.                (8.7)

Let y=A_k-s*q_k=(z-s)*q_k be the terminal radius. On the whole
integration interval from zero to that point, the radial derivative
is at most min(r,q_k): it is nondecreasing, equals q_k at y, and
is 1-Lipschitz from zero. Therefore

    2*(F(x_n)-min F)<=2*q_k*y-q_k^2
                         =(2*z-2*s-1)*q_k^2.           (8.8)

This bound explicitly allows arbitrary retained inner areas. It does
not set F(x_n) to the core value q_k^2/2. If the minimizer is an
interval, integrating from zero still gives exactly the minimum
normalization; any flat part only reduces the same upper integral.

For T=1+2*s and z>=s+1,

    (2*z-T)/z^2 <= 1/T,

because z^2-T*(2*z-T)=(z-T)^2>=0. Combining this identity with
(8.7)--(8.8) gives the full terminal bound

    V < D^2 / [(1+S_2)^2*(c+1)^(2*(k-1))*T].           (8.9)

It holds for the whole stated inward-shelf class. The frozen choice
z=T in (3.1) maximizes this scalar terminal ratio, but is not asserted
to maximize the actual payoff over all profiles.

Finally divide (8.9) by H_base and apply (2.5), k=m-2, and
D=a*c^(m-1). This gives

    V/H_base
      < [3*a^2*c^4/(1+S_2)^2]
                         *[c/(c+1)]^(2*(m-3))
      < 2*(25/49)^(m-3).                               (8.10)

The last inequalities are exact: a<1/2, c<5/2, and 1+S_2>4 imply
the leading factor is below 1875/1024<2; also c/(c+1)<5/7.
For m>=8, 25/49<2/3 gives
2*(25/49)^(m-3)<=2*(25/49)^5<64/243<1.

Our frozen law satisfies all the class hypotheses by Sections 3--6.
Consequently (8.1) and (8.10) establish (A)--(B), including actual
full-horizon error, the exact closest radius, and every history constraint.

## 9. The ORIGINAL all-split benchmark comparison

Use exactly the accepted definitions in fact 04219554e41d9750:

    Omega(u,v)=(u+v+sqrt((u+v)^2+4*u*v))/2,
    G(u,v)=2*u+v/2+sqrt(v^2+8*u*v)/2,
    C_0=B_0=1,
    C_n=max_(0<=r<n) Omega(C_r,C_(n-r-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=r<n) G(C_r,B_(n-r-1)),
    U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

Both maxima retain EVERY original integer split. That fact's
unconditional Section 7 gives

    B_(2^m-1)<=Z_1*lambda^m,
    Z_1=lim_(m->infinity) B_(2^m-1)/lambda^m in (0,infinity).

Here beta_word>=31/32 and c<5/2 give a>31/80. Since S_m>D,

    B_n*H_base < Z_1*lambda^m/(2*a*c^(m-1))
                 =Z_1*c/(2*a)*(lambda/c)^m
                 < (100/31)*Z_1.

Combining with (8.10), EVERY trajectory in the precise class of
Section 8 has

    0<=B_n*V<(200/31)*Z_1*(25/49)^(m-3) ->0.            (9.1)

In particular the chosen static family cannot attain 1/B_n at large
depth, or exceed H_base by any fixed factor. This is a result about
its actual payoff, not the weakness of a merely lower certificate.
No substituted balanced benchmark, benchmark DP, or evaluated ray
constant appears in the argument.

For comparison, H_base itself is attained on this exact word by the
single usual Huber derivative g_H(x)=min(x,1), started at 1+2*S_m.
All queries then lie above one, the last value is S_m+1/2, and the
true squared radius is (1+2*S_m)^2. This explicit check establishes
that (8.10) compares against an actual same-word witness.

## 10. What this does and does not settle

This round supplies a new depth-growing scalar knot policy, its full
static objective, global legality, integrated values, original physical
trajectory, exact minimum and closest radius, exact terminal payoff,
and a uniform quantitative obstruction for its entire stated inward
constant-gradient block mechanism. The old constant-gradient cells
cost distance through (8.3)--(8.7), even though their outer areas no
longer contribute at the eventual inner terminal point.

The function is not a fixed two-curvature homogeneous law or a
one-shelf law. Every added depth introduces another nonempty ramp and
shelf, and the actual trajectory queries all m-1 different magnitudes.
The knot spacings depend on the whole predetermined word. No fixed
start of 69b90f738a77a91b was retuned.

The obstruction requires the exact decomposition (2.3), constant
gradient on each closed physical block path, and disjoint inward
same-ray order (8.2). It does not cover overlapping same-ray returns,
mixed outward/inward records, or gradient variation within these
blocks. Those are explicit ways to escape its hypotheses, not a
claim that an escaping construction has useful payoff. Other starts
of the same constructed function need not satisfy these hypotheses
and are not excluded. Other checkpoint selections are not covered.

No statement here is an upper bound on W_n, on all scalar functions,
or on all possible m-dependent piecewise-affine gradients. A lower
witness on H_m would itself still leave the original all-word,
all-horizon minimax target. That target remains OPEN.

## 11. Local glossary and resources

The following symbols are local aliases, with their full definitions above.

* H_j,S_j: original enclosing-m word and its physical mass.
* d_j: level-j bridge excess a*c^j; 1+d_j is the physical step.
* k,b_i,s_i,D_i: number of selected checkpoints, their physical steps,
  intervening actual block masses, and b_i-1.
* s,D,T: S_(m-1), d_(m-1), and 1+2*s.
* q_i,A_i,R_i: stage gradient magnitude, starting radius, and radius
  after its whole ordinary block.
* Q_sigma: complete radial derivative on ray sign (-1)^sigma.
* U_i,l_i,E_in_i: actual ramp endpoint, shelf length, and retained inner
  shelf area on stage i's ray.
* g,F: single static derivative and its integral with minimum zero.
* P_i(r): mass of the first r original physical steps of block i.
* V,V_m,H_base: actual twice-gap/true-radius-squared payoff, its frozen
  value, and the exact same-word Huber payoff. Smoothness is one.
* z,y: terminal shelf starting radius divided by q_k, and terminal radius.
* Q_ij: project's signed directed smooth-convex interpolation residual.
* B_n,C_n,Z_1: ORIGINAL accepted all-split benchmark quantities.

One policy was frozen, and no repair was made. Resource counts:
mathematical programs 0; numerical evaluations 0; arithmetic replays 0;
executed recurrences 0; solvers 0; benchmark DP 0; fact_submit 0;
new accepted facts 0; installations 0; consults 0; native subagents 0.
Only primary retrieval, text/data reads and writes, and administrative
metadata checks were performed. Initial broad recall displays were

All new files lie in the assigned absolute public directory. Existing
private logs may be appended, but no new private artifact is created.
This is the handoff for the one authorized round; it does not renew
the completed59/attempted60 ceiling and does not authorize a formal
gate, publication, finalization, revocation, or shutdown.

# Coupled finite rows and depth retention: three complete analytical screens

JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT — UNVERIFIED. Worker max, invocation73 after assigned
completed baseline72; assigned attempted ceiling74. This is research evidence,
not a fact. There is no verifier submission, computational evaluation, actual
full-class upper bound, or resolution of the original exact-profile problem.

The three specified laws are: a pair of paid contact-neutral circulations;
a smooth depth ramp toward sustained inherited curvature; and immediate
mass-normalized retention after H4. Their complete arrays and entire-space
identities are below. The first law has two independent source restrictions
on the actual negative plane but pays one of them with the wrong sign. The
second starts positively on the entire plane, retains a uniformly positive
fraction of late inherited curvature, yet has already made the entire plane
negative by H9. The third starts positively on that plane at H5 but fails an
original initial-point mixed direction, including its Silver calibration.

## 1. Original target, fixed physical word, and authority

For every nonnegative physical word h of length n, W_n(h) is the supremum of
2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2), over every finite-dimensional real
Euclidean space, every convex differentiable L-smooth f with a minimizer
x_star, every L>0 and x_0!=x_star, with the actual updates
x_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)grad f(x_i). Thus W=2R. The original all-split benchmark is

    C_0=B_0=1,
    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,
    G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2,
    C_n=max_(0<=k<n)Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=k<n)G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

Every split is an original integer split. The affirmative goal requires a
uniform all-word, all-large-horizon lower comparison. The negative route
requires an actual unrestricted W upper with fixed relative improvement on
unbounded actual horizons. No failed dual is a primal lower witness.

Fix ONE enclosing integer m>=1536 and set

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    Hword_0=(), Hword_(j+1)=(Hword_j,1+a*c^j,Hword_j),
    A_j=1+sum Hword_j, d_j=a*c^j, D_j=2A_j-1.

The actual word is Hword_m=H_(m,1/4) of length n_m=2^m-1. Every child
inherits the SAME m and epsilon. Hword_j is used here to distinguish the
physical word from the conjugate quadratic H_j of fact1252b8823bb5a290.
The diagnostic epsilon=0 is the original Silver word; it is never substituted
for the assigned positive epsilon. All displayed recurrences are finite
mathematical definitions or symbolic identities, never executed programs.

Accepted inputs are fact1252b8823bb5a290 (original interpolation and formal
space), fact04219554e41d9750 Sections10--11 (primitive/history and full-space
composition conventions), and fact5fd396eba75651e7 Section10 (ONLY the
unconditional scalar benchmark comparison). Full findings9864512779c0d768,
23ca982e95eefa1f and70884c07cdfd260a and main elaboration0d9770af2df0344c were
read as awareness, not predecessors. All unverified formulas needed here are
derived together in this draft. None is promoted or reused as verified truth.

Normalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. Retain independent original formal
vectors x_0,g_0,...,g_n, including every actual bridge and the unqueried
terminal, and every ordered finite and optimum row. Put

    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, S=sum h, D=1+2S, h_n=0.

Actual interpolation gives Q_ij<=0 by fact1252. Write K_ij for finite
multipliers, nu_i for i->star, xi_i for star->i, with K_ii=0. Every law
below has xi_i=0. All FINAL multipliers must be nonnegative. The exact value
flow and complete objective matrix are

    sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+nu_i=2*1_(i=n),                    (1)
    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i!=j)K_ij Q_ij
                              +sum_i nu_i Q_(i,star)
        =q||x_0||^2-sum_i nu_i<x_0,g_i>+C(g),               (2)
    C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i*sum_(j>i)K_ij,
    2C_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j*sum_(v>j)K_iv
                         +h_i[nu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j,
    out_i=nu_i+sum_j K_ij.                                 (3)

Here C(g)=sum_(i,j)C_ij<g_i,g_j>. The full matrix is
[[q,-nu^T/2],[-nu/2,C]]. To derive (3), half the incident mass is
out_i-1_(i=n) by (1); forward position rows subtract h_i K_ij from the
source diagonal. Difference squares and the two prefix position terms give
the three displayed off-diagonal contributions. Thus (1)--(3) retain ALL
initial-point and gradient directions. Full PSD would imply W<=q, with no
quotient or child-feasibility assumption.

The original Huber assignment x_0=D,g_i=1,f_i=D-Ppos_i-1/2 makes M(1/D)=0.
Its contact equations, including the mixed derivatives, are

    (2S-Ppos_i)nu_i=2h_i+sum_j K_ij(Ppos_i-Ppos_j).          (4)

Indeed twice row i's sum in C is
2h_i+(1+Ppos_i)nu_i+sum_j K_ij(Ppos_i-Ppos_j). All arrays below obey (4).
Raising q adds only (q-1/D)||x_0||^2; it cannot fix a test with x_0=0.

## 2. Complete source array and its actual two-dimensional negative plane

Let z be a child terminal and define the full primitive history

    V_j=2sum_(i<z)h_i(f_i-f_z)
       +sum_(i<z)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-A_j(A_j-1)||g_z||^2.

Starting from Lambda^0=0, at the actual join from Hword_j to Hword_(j+1)
embed Lambda^j on the left with weight1 and on the right with weight k_j.
With bridge p and terminal z, add exactly

    u_j sum_(i right,i<z)h_i Q_(i,p)
     +v_j sum_(i right,i<z)h_i Q_(i,z)
     +w_j Q_(z,p)+z_j Q_(p,z),
    u_j=2lambda,
    v_j=[u_j d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,
    k_j=1+(u_j+v_j)/2,
    z_j=2A_j,
    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-u_j(A_j-1)
                           =2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda).    (5)

Aggregate repeated actual rows. The identities u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1) and
u_j(A_j-1)+w_j-z_j=2(A_j+d_j) give divergence -2h_i at nonterminals and
2(A_(j+1)-1) at the terminal. Define the full gradient polynomial by

    V_j+sum_(i!=v)Lambda^j_iv Q_iv=Psrc_j(g).                (6)

At a join set t_j=d_j/A_j, t_star=1/lambda,
r=sum_(i right,i<z)h_i g_i, Y=r-(A_j-1)g_z, Z=g_p-g_z. Full expansion gives

    Psrc_(j+1)=Psrc_j^left+k_j Psrc_j^right+Ejoin_j,
    Ejoin_j=(t_star-t_j)[(lambda+1)||Y||^2
        -2lambda A_j<Y,Z>+A_j^2(2+t_star+t_j)||Z||^2].       (7)

For a complete audit, the four unweighted gradient polynomials of the new
rows in (5) are

    d_j<r,g_p>+||r||^2/2
       +sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2+(A_j-1)||g_p||^2/2;
    -||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2
       +(A_j-1)||g_z||^2/2-<r,g_z>;
    d_j<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;
    -(d_j+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2-<g_p,r>-<g_p,g_z>.

The ||r||^2,r*g_p,r*g_z coefficients are (u_j-v_j)/2,
u_j d_j-z_j, w_j-v_j. The remaining bridge and terminal diagonals are

    A_j(A_j-1)-d_j-d_j^2+[u_j(A_j-1)+w_j]/2-(d_j+1/2)z_j,
    -A_(j+1)(A_(j+1)-1)+k_j A_j(A_j-1)
                                     +v_j(A_j-1)/2+(w_j+z_j)/2.

Substitution yields (7) on the whole original gradient space. Its bracket
is positive definite in (Y,Z): the first diagonal is positive and the
determinant divided by A_j^2 is
(lambda+1)(2+t_star+t_j)-lambda^2>=lambda>0.

Put v_h=a/(c-2). Direct summation of the mathematical word gives

    0<v_h<1,
    A_j=v_h c^j+(1-v_h)2^j,
    t_j=(c-2)/[1+(1/v_h-1)(2/c)^j].                      (8)

Here c(c-2)>1>beta, so t_j increases and A_j<c^j for j>0. At the first
levels,

    t_0<t_star, t_1<t_star, t_2>t_star,
    lambda d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0,
    c(lambda d_2-A_2)=epsilon[(lambda+4)
                       -(4lambda+2)epsilon-(5lambda+2)epsilon^2]>4epsilon.

Since c<5/2, lambda d_2-A_2>epsilon. Every sign in (5) holds: u,v,k,z
are positive, w_0=4+2a>0, A_1<lambda gives w_1>2lambda-2>0, and
t_j>t_star gives w_j>2lambda for j>=2. At epsilon=0, Psrc_j=0 identically
by (7); for all parameters Psrc_j annihilates common gradients.

Define the LOCAL support lengths and deficit

    b=1+a, Wloc=A_2+d_2, Uloc=A_2+d_3,
    Tloc=Wloc+Uloc=A_3+d_3,
    Edef=(lambda d_2-A_2)(A_2+d_2/lambda)>epsilon,
    delta3=t_3-t_star>0.                                (9)

Wloc is unrelated to the worst-case functional W_n. On the ORIGINAL H4
gradient space assign scalars

    g_i=u for i=0,1,2,3;
    g_i=v0 for i=4,5,6,7;
    g_i=w for i=8,9,10,11;
    g_i=0 for i=12,13,14,15.                             (10)

These are test assignments, not formal-coordinate identifications. Applying
(7) to the two H3 children and their H4 join gives

    Psrc_4=-Edef(u-v0)^2-k_3 Edef w^2
       -delta3[(lambda+1)Wloc^2 w^2-2lambda A_3 Wloc w v0
                           +A_3^2(2+t_star+t_3)v0^2].   (11)

The left H3 has Y=0,Z=u-v0 and right H3 gives -Edef*w^2; the H4 join has
Y=Wloc*w,Z=v0. This proves every term of (11). It is strictly negative
for every nonzero (u,v0,w). The total physical displacement is

    Wloc(u+w)+Uloc*v0.                                  (12)

Let N be the kernel of (12) in this three-dimensional test subspace. It has
dimension2 and terminal gradient0. On N write

    rhoN=Tloc/Wloc, u=-w-(Uloc/Wloc)v0,
    u-v0=-w-rhoN*v0, normN=w^2+v0^2.                    (13)

Every nonzero vector of N has normN>0. Placing N in the FIRST actual H4
of any Hword_r, r>=4, and setting all later gradients zero preserves
Psrc_r=Psrc_4: each higher source join has Y=Z=0 and left weight1.

The following written bounds will also be used:

    29/5<A_2<6, 12/5<d_2<121/50, 29/5<d_3<6,
    14<A_3<721/50, 41/5<Wloc<9, 58/5<Uloc<12,
    2<rhoN<5/2, 60<D_4<1717/25<80, D_3<29,
    0<Edef<24epsilon, 0<delta3<3epsilon/4.              (14)

Indeed A_2=lambda^2-4epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)]-epsilon,
d_2=lambda(1-epsilon^2),
d_3=lambda^2(1+epsilon-epsilon^2-epsilon^3). The rational enclosures
141/100<sqrt(2)<71/50 imply the first lengths; A_3=2A_2+d_2 gives the
next bounds. The earlier first-level formula gives lambda d_2-A_2<3epsilon
and A_2+d_2/lambda<8. Finally

    lambda d_3-A_3
      =epsilon[lambda^3+8/(lambda(1+epsilon))+2]
        +epsilon^2(lambda-lambda^3)-lambda^3 epsilon^3
      <21epsilon,

so delta3<21epsilon/(lambda A_3)<3epsilon/4. No arithmetic replay is used.
From (11)--(14), on the ENTIRE N,

    Psrc_4<-(epsilon/3)normN,
    |Psrc_4|<2000epsilon*normN.                         (15)

For the first bound, drop the final negative bracket and use k_3>1.
Since rhoN>1, v0^2<=2(w+rhoN v0)^2+2w^2, proving the factor1/3.
For the second, k_3<7 follows from u_3<6,v_3<6. The two Edef terms
are at most24epsilon[13normN+7w^2]<=480epsilon normN. The bracket's
absolute value is at most
324w^2+810|w v0|+675v0^2<=1080normN; multiply by delta3<3epsilon/4.
The sum is below2000epsilon normN. These are full quadratic estimates
on the plane, not tests of two separate vectors.

## 3. Every contextual finite coefficient and the full original identity

At a join from Hword_j to Hword_(j+1), let k=2^j-1, use labels L_l=l,
0<=l<=k, bridge p=L_k, and R_i=k+1+i, 0<=i<=k, terminal R_k. Put

    A=A_j-1, d=1+d_j, V=3A+2d,
    S=2A+d, D=D_(j+1), D_c=D_j,
    s_j=(A+d)/V=(A_j+d_j)/(3A_j+2d_j-1), theta_j=1-s_j,
    x_p=x_0-sum_(l<k)h_l g_(L_l).

The child has finite array K^c, attachments mu_i, sum mu_i=2 and xi=0.
Initialize Hword_0 with K^c=0,mu_0=2. For ANY specified gamma_j in[0,1]
set all parent coefficients to

    K_(L_l,L_v)=(1-gamma_j)Lambda^j_lv/D+gamma_j K^c_lv
                         +2gamma_j s_j mu_l*1_(v=k,l<k),
    K_(R_i,R_v)=K^c_iv,
    K_(R_i,L_k)=s_j mu_i, all other cross rows zero,
    nu_(L_l)=(1-gamma_j)2h_l/D+gamma_j(A/V)mu_l, l<k,
    nu_(L_k)=(1-gamma_j)(2s_j-2A/D)
                          +gamma_j(2d+A mu_k)/V,
    nu_(R_i)=theta_j mu_i, xi_i=0.                       (16)

This is a complete recursive array definition; there is no unassigned Schur
perturbation. The source array for law A will be replaced explicitly in
Section5. No child PSD is presumed in any screen.

All final signs hold. In particular
(A+d)D-A(3A+2d)=A^2+4Ad+2d^2+A+d>0 pays the bridge attachment
at gamma0; at gamma1 all attachments and finite rows are nonnegative.
For A>0, 1/3<s_j<1/2, while s_0=1/2. A convex combination retains signs.

At gamma0 the left source divergence -2h_l/D is canceled by its optimum
attachments and the total incoming backward mass2s_j; at the bridge use
source divergence2A/D. At gamma1 child divergence is2*1_(l=k)-mu_l.
The extra forward mass2s_j mu_l and attachment(1-2s_j)mu_l cancel every
nonterminal coefficient, and the bridge balances the incoming mass2s_j.
Right flow is child flow plus(s_j+theta_j)mu_i. Thus (1) holds and total
left attachment is2s_j. These calculations prove full value flow for (16).

For left nonterminal contact, subtraction of child (4) at gamma1 gives
V*forward_l=2(A+d)mu_l, hence forward_l=2s_j mu_l. At gamma0 source
annihilation of common gradients and the primitive completion below give
contact. Right contact uses theta_j V=2A+d. The remaining bridge derivative
follows from the other gradient derivatives, the initial derivative and
M(D,ones)=0 by homogeneity. Thus full contact (4) holds at every depth.

Define r_L=sum_l mu_l g_(L_l), r_R=sum_i mu_i g_(R_i), and

    Rchild_R=C^c(g_R)-D_c||r_R||^2/4,
    kappa_j=D_c-D theta_j^2.

These are definitions; no positivity of Rchild_R or kappa_j is assumed.
The full gamma0 and gamma1 quadratics at q=1/D are

    M_I=Psrc_j(g_L)/D+Rchild_R
       +||x_p+(A-D s_j)g_p-(D theta_j/2)r_R||^2/D
       +(kappa_j/4)||r_R-2g_p||^2,                    (17)

    M_O=||x_0||^2/D+C^c(g_L)+C^c(g_R)-<x_0,r_L>
       +2s_j<r_L,x_p-g_p>-(6s_j-2)<g_p,x_p>
       -theta_j<x_p,r_R>+(d-s_j)<g_p,r_R>
       +(6s_j-1)||g_p||^2.                            (18)

For (17), the primitive source plus left optimum rows gives Psrc_j/D and
||x_p-(A+1)g_p||^2/D. Replacing right optimum rows by
theta_j Q_(R_i,star)+s_j Q_(R_i,p) changes them by
s_j[-f_p+<g_(R_i),x_p-g_p>+||g_p||^2/2]. After cancellation the remaining
quadratic in x_p,g_p,r_R is

    ||x_p||^2/D+2(A/D-s_j)<x_p,g_p>+(A^2/D+2s_j)||g_p||^2
       -theta_j<x_p,r_R>+(d-s_j)<g_p,r_R>+D_c||r_R||^2/4.

Completion using s_j V=A+d is exactly (17). For (18) the left correction
is 2s_j sum_l mu_l(Q_(L_l,p)-Q_(L_l,star))+(6s_j-2)Q_(p,star), including
l=k with Q_(p,p)=0. The difference is
-f_p+<g_(L_l),x_p-g_p>+||g_p||^2/2. It cancels the right replacement's
value -2s_j f_p, and its gradient terms together with that replacement
give (18). This audits all finite, optimum, terminal and x_0 mixed terms.
Consequently the ENTIRE original identity for (16) is

    M_gamma(q)=(1-gamma_j)M_I+gamma_j M_O
                                      +(q-1/D)||x_0||^2. (19)

## 4. Common H2 gates, Silver calibration, and the ENTIRE initial N form

On Hword_1, K_(1,0)=1 and nu_0=nu_1=1 for every gamma_0, and

    M_1(1/D_1)=||x_0-D_1(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/D_1
                          +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4,
    Psrc_1=(2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2.                        (20)

Both are nonnegative since b<sqrt(2)<3/2. For H2, put d=2-epsilon,
D=1+4b+2d, alpha=b/(3b+2d). The ORIGINAL index0 mixed Schur value for
gamma_1=epsilon/4 is exactly

    C_00-D nu_0^2/4=(2-b^2)/D
       +gamma_1[1-b+2(b^2-1)/D]
       -(D/4)gamma_1^2(alpha-2b/D)^2>epsilon/30.        (21)

Indeed 2-b^2>2epsilon,D<11, the bracket>-1/2, the last term<epsilon/1000,
giving epsilon(2/11-1/8-1/1000)>epsilon/30. This includes the original
x_0 mixed coefficient. The bridge's gamma0 Schur value is
kappa_1+(2-b^2)/D. Bounds b<10/7,d>199/100 give theta_1<59/100,
D<75/7,D_c>19/5, hence kappa_1>19/5-15/4=1/20. Changing to epsilon/4
uses bridge endpoint coefficients
C_I=2s_1+2/D,C_O=4s_1,
nu_I=2s_1-2b/D,nu_O=(2d+b)/(3b+2d).
Their C difference is2s_1-2/D with absolute value below1, and their nu
difference is2b/D-alpha with absolute value below1; both old attachments
are below1. Thus the change alters C and nu by less than gamma_1 and the Schur value by less than
7gamma_1<1/1000. Right indices2,3 have C_ii=1,nu_i=theta_1 and positive
Schur value [4-D_c+kappa_1]/4. Thus laws B and C pass all four specified
H2 isolated-gradient mixed tests. These diagonal tests do not assert full
actual H2 PSD. Law A has gamma0 and no source change through H2: (17),
Psrc_1>=0,kappa_1>0 and the full H1 Schur remainder prove its full H2 PSD.

For the gamma0 Silver calibration, write u_star=1/lambda. The physical
bridge is d=1+u_star(A+1), with u_star^2+2u_star=1, and direct expansion gives

    V^2 kappa_j=2(1-u_star)A^3+(6-8u_star)A^2
       +(1+u_star)(8u_star-2)A+(1+u_star)^2(1-2u_star)>0. (22)

Every coefficient is positive because1/4<u_star<1/2. At epsilon=0,
Psrc_j=0. From the empty full certificate ||x_0-g_0||^2 and (17), the
entire child Schur remainder and the retained squares prove full Silver
PSD at every gamma0 depth. Laws A and B specialize exactly to this law;
law C's later different gamma is screened separately, not certified by (22).

We now derive an ENTIRE plane form for ANY contextual law (16). Let
s=s_2, theta=1-s, define

    aN=2s A_2,
    bN=s(2Wloc-1), cN=D_3 theta-bN,
    r_L=2s u+2theta v0, r_R=2s w,
    Ldisp=Wloc*u+(A_2-1)v0,
    x_p=-Ldisp=Wloc*w+(1+d_3)v0 on N,
    zN=s*w-v0, Rblock=aN-D_3 s^2.

The H3 child quadratic compressed to its two common groups is

    C^c(u,v0)=aN*u^2+2bN*u*v0+cN*v0^2
            =D_3(s*u+theta*v0)^2+Rblock(u-v0)^2.        (23)

To prove (23) WITHOUT child PSD, all left-group gradients equal1 and all
right gradients0 give C_left,left=2s A_2 directly from either (17) or (18).
Group attachment masses are2s,2theta by (16); contact row sums then force
C_left,right=s(D_3-2A_2)=bN and C_right,right=D_3 theta-bN. Subtracting
D_3(su+theta v0)^2 leaves Rblock(u-v0)^2. In particular the right child
with common first group w and second group0 has C^c=aN*w^2.

Writing A=A_3-1,d=1+d_3, the entire H4 forms on N are

    I_N=-Edef(u-v0)^2/D_4+Rblock*w^2
       +[(Wloc-D_4 theta_3 s)w+(Tloc-D_4 s_3)v0]^2/D_4
       +kappa_3 zN^2,                                 (24)

    O_N=C^c(u,v0)+aN*w^2
       +2s_3 r_L(Wloc*w+d_3*v0)-(6s_3-2)Wloc*v0*w
       -theta_3 Wloc*w*r_R+s_3*d_3*v0*r_R
       +[1-(6s_3-2)d_3]v0^2,
    F_4=(1-gamma_3)I_N+gamma_3 O_N.                    (25)

Substitution into the FULL identities (17)--(18) proves (24)--(25).
There is no conclusion from two chosen plane vectors. Uniformly for
0<=gamma_3<=4epsilon, the complete quadratic bounds are

    F_4>(w^2+zN^2)/12>=normN/24,
    F_4<1000normN.                                    (26)

Here are the details. The numerator of 2A_2-D_3 s is
2A_2^2-2A_2 d_2-2d_2^2-A_2+d_2. From (14) it exceeds
2(29/5)^2-12(121/50)-2(121/50)^2-6+12/5>22; its denominator
3A_2+2d_2-1 is below22. Since s>1/3, Rblock>1/3. Also 2d_3+3>A_3 gives
s_3>3/8,theta_3<5/8. Therefore

    kappa_3>27-(1717/25)(25/64)=11/64>1/6.

Use u-v0=-w-rhoN*v0, v0=s*w-zN, rhoN<3 and s<1/2. Then
(u-v0)^2<25w^2/2+18zN^2. Dropping the positive square in (24) gives

    I_N>(1/3-5epsilon)w^2+(1/6-36epsilon/5)zN^2
                              >w^2/4+zN^2/8.          (27)

The child terms in O_N are nonnegative by the explicit (23) and Rblock>0,
not by full child PSD. Since |r_L|<=|w|+3|v0|/2, Wloc<9, d<7 and
1/3<s_3<1/2, its remaining terms give

    O_N>=-18w^2-(63/2)|w*v0|-14v0^2
         >=-(135/4)w^2-(119/4)v0^2
         >-60(w^2+zN^2).                              (28)

The last line uses v0^2<=w^2/2+2zN^2. Combining (27)--(28) costs at most
241epsilon on either w^2 or zN^2 when gamma_3<=4epsilon. Because
241epsilon<=241/6144<1/24, (26)'s lower bound follows. Also
normN<=2(w^2+zN^2), proving its second comparison.
For the upper bound, aN<6, bN<9,cN<29 and u^2<=2w^2+9v0^2/2 imply
C^c(u,v0)<106normN. In (24), the square has absolute linear coefficients
below65 and D_4>60, so its value is below141normN; kappa_3<D_3<29 and
zN^2<=2normN give I_N<250normN. The absolute remaining terms of (25)
are bounded respectively by19,5,9,2,5 times normN, and aN*w^2<6normN.
Thus |O_N|<200normN and the loose upper1000normN holds. This completes
the whole-plane gate with every mixed coefficient accounted for.

## 5. Law A: two independent paid circulations, with an exact sign failure

Use gamma_j=0 in (16). Change the actual H4 SOURCE as follows, keeping
Lambda^j unchanged through H3. Define the original position differences

    ell_pair=Ppos_7-Ppos_5=b+(2-epsilon),
    r_pair=Ppos_5-Ppos_2=2b+(1+d_2),
    T_pair=ell_pair+r_pair=Ppos_7-Ppos_2.

The first circulation has sources3,6 and destinations2,5,7:

    DeltaL_3,2=-tau_pairL*ell_pair,
    DeltaL_3,5= tau_pairL*T_pair,
    DeltaL_3,7=-tau_pairL*r_pair,
    DeltaL_6,2= tau_pairL*ell_pair,
    DeltaL_6,5=-tau_pairL*T_pair,
    DeltaL_6,7= tau_pairL*r_pair,
    tau_pairL=(epsilon/2)*min{
          k_1*w_0/ell_pair, 2A_2/r_pair, k_2*u_1*b/T_pair}.  (29)

Every subtracted original entry is explicitly

    Lambda^4_3,2=k_1*w_0,
    Lambda^4_3,7=2A_2,
    Lambda^4_6,5=k_2*u_1*b.                              (30)

For the first, take the terminal->initial edge w_0 in the right H1 of
the left H2, with scaling k_1; its H3 and H4 embeddings are left embeddings
of weight1. The second is the H3 bridge->terminal entry z_2. For the third,
take the H2 right nonterminal->bridge row u_1*b in the right H2 of H3 and
scale by k_2. No other source rule adds to these entries. All are positive.
Thus each removal is at most epsilon/2 of its available entry.

The second circulation has sources10,12 and destinations7,11,15:

    DeltaR_10,7= tau_pairR*Wloc, DeltaR_10,11=-tau_pairR*Tloc,
    DeltaR_10,15=tau_pairR*Uloc, DeltaR_12,7=-tau_pairR*Wloc,
    DeltaR_12,11=tau_pairR*Tloc, DeltaR_12,15=-tau_pairR*Uloc,
    tau_pairR=(epsilon/2)*min{
         k_3(2k_1+v_1*b)/Tloc, u_3*b/Wloc,
                                  (k_3*v_2+v_3)*b/Uloc}.  (31)

The exact subtracted entries are
Lambda^4_10,11=k_3(2k_1+v_1*b), Lambda^4_12,7=u_3*b,
Lambda^4_12,15=(k_3*v_2+v_3)b. The first combines the nonterminal->terminal
edge2 of its right H1 with the H2 v_1*b row; the outer H4 gives k_3.
The second is the actual H4 backward source row. The third combines its
inherited H3 v_2*b terminal transport and its new H4 v_3*b terminal row.
Again all removals are at most epsilon/2 of the stated positive originals.

Set Lambda_pair^4=Lambda^4+DeltaL+DeltaR, and for j>=4 use (5) with the
inherited left Lambda_pair^j and right k_j Lambda_pair^j, with no additional
root correction. This places the two circulations once in EACH original
dyadic H4, with positive ancestral right weights. Their decremented rows
have disjoint source labels and are not charged twice. All final finite
coefficients are nonnegative. All other objective coefficients are EXACTLY
(16) at gamma0 with Lambda_pair instead of Lambda. This defines the whole
law, including all final optimum and terminal signs.

Both circulations have zero row sums and zero column sums. Their destination
vectors also have zero physical first moment:

    ell_pair Ppos_2-T_pair Ppos_5+r_pair Ppos_7=0,
    Wloc Ppos_7-Tloc Ppos_11+Uloc Ppos_15=0.

Therefore they preserve every original value-flow coefficient and contact
moment. With the original proximal points y_i=x_i-g_i of fact1252, their
ENTIRE original-space polynomial changes are

    DeltaP_L=tau_pairL<g_6-g_3,
                        ell_pair*y_2-T_pair*y_5+r_pair*y_7>,
    DeltaP_R=tau_pairR<g_10-g_12,
                        Wloc*y_7-Tloc*y_11+Uloc*y_15>.     (32)

To check (32), expand sum c_i d_j Q_ij. Both coefficient sums vanish,
canceling all values, source positions and pure squares; the remaining
term is <sum c_i g_i,sum d_j(x_j-g_j)>. Each factor vanishes on common
gradients, so the entire gradient derivative at contact also vanishes.
The full original matrix is (2)--(3) with these rows, and its lifted identity
is exactly (17) with the changed Psrc. No new off-diagonal is suppressed.

Writing Csrc for the gradient matrix of Psrc, the first circulation has
negative isolated SOURCE diagonal changes

    DeltaCsrc_3,3=-tau_pairL*ell_pair*(1+d_2),
    DeltaCsrc_6,6=-tau_pairL*r_pair*b.

Every other DeltaL diagonal is zero. The second has positive changes
DeltaCsrc_10,10=tau_pairR*Wloc*b and
DeltaCsrc_12,12=tau_pairR*Uloc*b, with other DeltaR diagonals zero.
These signs are explicitly retained, not treated as a diagonal PSD cure.
H2 is unchanged and passes the full gate from Section4. At epsilon=0 both
circulations vanish, giving the full Silver calibration (22).

Now evaluate the ENTIRE actual plane. On (10),

    y_2=-(ell_pair+1)u,
    y_5=-Wloc*u-(b+1)v0,
    y_7=-Wloc*u-A_2*v0,
    ell_pair*y_2-T_pair*y_5+r_pair*y_7
                                  =ell_pair(b+d_2)(u-v0).

The last equality follows since Wloc-ell_pair-1=b+d_2 and
b+1-A_2=-ell_pair. Also on N, y_15=0 and
y_11=-Wloc*u-Uloc*v0-A_2*w. Consequently

    Ppair_4|N=Psrc_4|N
       -tau_pairL*ell_pair(b+d_2)(u-v0)^2
       +tau_pairR[(A_2^2-d_2*d_3)w^2+Wloc*d_3*w*v0].    (33)

This is the full two-variable quadratic, including the new cross term.
The source restrictions phiL=v0-u and phiR=w are jointly injective on N:
if both vanish, (12) forces Tloc*v0=0 and hence u=v0=w=0. Independence
does not make (33) nonnegative. At the EXACT original vector

    v0=1, w=0, u=-Uloc/Wloc,                            (34)

both the baseline (11) and the first correction are strictly negative,
while the second correction is zero. More explicitly,

    Ppair_4(34)=-(Edef+tau_pairL ell_pair(b+d_2))rhoN^2
                        -delta3 A_3^2(2+t_star+t_3)<0.   (35)

This is the previously missed mode; the new independent left source is
nonzero there, but its paid orientation worsens it. Reversing DeltaL would
make its plane square positive, but would subtract from the EXACT ZERO
entries Lambda^4_3,5 and Lambda^4_6,2. Their zero status follows from (5):
the H3 bridge3 links outside its left child only to terminal7, and right
source6 links outside its right child only to bridge3 and terminal7.
Thus reversing this channel violates final signs for every positive size.

Place (34) in the first H4 of the left child of Hword_m, every other original
gradient zero and x_0=0. Upper source joins have zero Y,Z, and their other
H4 circulations have zero source gradients. The top objective has x_p=0,
g_p=0 and all right gradients zero, so EVERY term of the full lift except
Ppair_4/D_m vanishes. Therefore

    M_A(q)=Ppair_4(34)/D_m<0 for EVERY real q.             (36)

This is a paid two-source law with joint injectivity that nevertheless fails
the full original matrix. It does not exclude other multiple-source laws.

## 6. Law B: a smooth depth ramp with sustained late curvature, defeated on ALL N

Keep the original source (5) and specify EVERY contextual gamma in (16) by

    gamma_0=gamma_1=epsilon/4,
    gamma_j=(D_j/D_(j+1))*[1-(1-epsilon)^(2^j)], j>=2.    (37)

All gamma are in[0,1], so all final signs, flow and contact are already paid
by Section3. There are no new signed rows or undefined coefficient choices.
At epsilon=0 EVERY gamma is zero. Thus this law passes the original H2
mixed tests (21) and the full all-depth Silver calibration (22).
At H4, gamma_3<(1/2)*8epsilon=4epsilon; (24)--(28) therefore prove that
the ENTIRE actual N has positive F_4, not just two tested basis vectors.

For r>=4 define F_r to be this whole law's M_r on the first original H4
test N, later gradients zero and x_0=0. Define Tplane_r=D_r F_r. With
P_N=Psrc_4|N, the FULL original identities (17)--(19) give

    F_(r+1)=gamma_r F_r+(1-gamma_r)P_N/D_(r+1),
    Tplane_(r+1)=hold_r Tplane_r+(1-gamma_r)P_N,
    hold_r=1-(1-epsilon)^(2^r), r>=4.                   (38)

The source survives unchanged because displacement and terminal are zero;
the primitive endpoint's square and right child terms vanish. The objective
endpoint leaves exactly the left child quadratic F_r. This derives (38)
without child PSD or an executed recurrence. It is an identity of quadratic
forms on the entire plane. Any q has the same restriction since x_0=0.

Bernoulli's inequality gives hold_r<=2^r epsilon. The full initial bound
(26) and D_4<80 give Tplane_4<80000normN. Expanding the FIVE symbolic
identities for r=4,...,8, every inserted source term is negative by (15).
Keeping only the last one yields

    Tplane_9<80000epsilon^5 2^30 normN+(1-gamma_8)P_N
             <80000epsilon^5 2^30 normN-epsilon normN/6. (39)

Here gamma_8<D_8/D_9<1/2. Since epsilon<=1/(3*2^11),

    80000epsilon^4 2^30<=80000/(81*2^14)<1/6;

the last strict comparison is the written integer inequality
480000<81*16384. It was not evaluated by a program. Thus (39) is strictly
negative for EVERY nonzero point of N. Every later (38) has hold_r>0 and
(1-gamma_r)P_N<0, so the ENTIRE N remains strictly negative, in particular

    M_B(q)|N=F_m<0 for EVERY real q, m>=1536.             (40)

The positive H4 plane has been lost already at the finite H9 gate. There
is no proposed deep PSD induction after that failure; (40) is simply the
original-space embedding and sign-preserving identity.

This failure is not a restatement of constant epsilon/4 attenuation. The
law has a quantitatively sustained late inheritance at the natural
objective-mass normalization. Let Jret be the least integer with2^Jret>=16m.
Then Jret<=m-1 for m>=1536; the elementary inequality2^(m-1)>=16m holds
already at m=16 and is preserved when m increases. For r=Jret+t,

    1-hold_r=(1-epsilon)^(2^r)
        <=1/(1+epsilon 2^r)<1/(4*2^t).

For the first inequality, apply Bernoulli to
(1-epsilon)^(-2^r)=[1+epsilon/(1-epsilon)]^(2^r). Therefore, for EVERY
suffix endpoint k with Jret<=k<=m-1,

    product_(r=Jret)^k hold_r
       >=1-sum_(r=Jret)^k(1-hold_r)>1/2.                (41)

The finite product inequality follows inductively from(1-x)(1-y)>=1-x-y
for x,y in[0,1]. Equation (41) retains more than half of the normalized
inherited late curvature over the ENTIRE suffix, with no child reset.
It cannot revive a plane already negative at H9. No whole-word margin,
full PSD, or unrestricted W upper is obtained.

## 7. Law C: immediate normalized retention pays N at H5 and loses the mixed test

Keep the original source (5) and use the DIFFERENT explicit contextual law

    gamma_j=epsilon/4 for0<=j<=3,
    gamma_j=D_j/D_(j+1) forj>=4.                        (42)

All final coefficients are exactly (16) and sign-valid; full flow/contact
and the original matrix identity are unchanged. H2 passes every specified
original mixed gate. H4 obeys the full-plane bounds (26) since gamma_3 is
epsilon/4. At the first retaining join, (38)'s general identity now has
normalized inheritance coefficient1. Thus on ALL N,

    Tplane_5=Tplane_4+(1-gamma_4)P_N
       >(60/24-2000epsilon)normN>(13/6)normN>0.         (43)

The second inequality uses2000/6144<1/3. This is a complete positive
two-dimensional compression at H5; it is not claimed full PSD. For clarity,
the actual whole-word plane restriction of the pre-specified law is exactly

    Tplane_m=Tplane_4+P_N sum_(j=4)^(m-1)(1-D_j/D_(j+1)). (44)

No positivity of (44) is asserted. The full matrix is already rejected by
the following independent mixed direction, so no deep PSD induction is used.

Let nu0_j and C00_j be the ORIGINAL index0 entries of (2)--(3) for Hword_j.
The first physical step is b at every nonempty depth. For j>=1 evaluate (17)--(18) with only
the original gradient g_0 nonzero, every other gradient zero. In the primitive
endpoint Psrc_j=(2-b^2)||g_0||^2 and x_p=x_0-b*g_0. This gives

    nu0_(j+1)=(1-gamma_j)2b/D_(j+1)
                                    +gamma_j(1-2s_j)nu0_j,
    C00_(j+1)=(1-gamma_j)2/D_(j+1)
                               +gamma_j(C00_j-2s_j b nu0_j). (45)

These are exact ORIGINAL matrix entries, not a child-PSD argument. Define

    Kmix=2(b^2-1)>0,
    R0_j=D_j(b*nu0_j-C00_j),
    R0_1=D_1(b-1)>0.

Taking b times the first line of(45) minus its second yields the scalar
identity

    R0_(j+1)=(D_(j+1)/D_j)gamma_j R0_j+(1-gamma_j)Kmix. (46)

It is derived textually and not executed. For the ORIGINAL full-space test

    x_0=b, g_0=1, all other original gradients zero,
    q=zeta/D_j,

one has exactly

    M_j(zeta/D_j)=(zeta*b^2-R0_j)/D_j.                 (47)

Thus the actual mixed coefficient remains load-bearing even though the
whole plane N at H5 has just passed (43).

For law (42), R0_4>(1-epsilon/4)Kmix by (46). Since D_4/D_5<1/2,

    R0_5=R0_4+(1-D_4/D_5)Kmix
       >(3/2-epsilon/4)Kmix>14/5.                     (48)

The last bound follows from b>7/5, Kmix>48/25 and epsilon<=1/6144.
For every r>=5, (46) at gamma=D_r/D_(r+1) increases R0_r strictly.
If zeta<=11/10, b<3/2 gives zeta*b^2<99/40. Consequently, at EVERY
actual depth r>=5, including the original assigned m,

    M_r(zeta/D_r)<-13/(40D_r)<0.                      (49)

This is the entire original x_0,g_0 compression with its exact physical
step, not an isolated gradient diagonal. The certificate law cannot give
the required zeta<250/237<11/10 on the actual word.

There is also an EXACT whole-word loss requirement. From (46) and (42),

    R0_m=R0_4+Kmix sum_(j=4)^(m-1)(1-D_j/D_(j+1))
         >(m-4)Kmix/2.

Every PSD level in this SPECIFIED law must satisfy (47)>=0, hence

    zeta>=R0_m/b^2>(m-4)(1-b^(-2))>(24/49)(m-4).       (50)

This is the full-word required inflation, not a per-level allowance sold as
a bounded total margin. No statement about unrestricted W follows from it.

Finally this law fails its relevant Silver calibration already at H5.
At epsilon=0, the earlier gamma through H4 is0, b=sqrt(2), Kmix=2, and
R0_4=2. The prescribed retention then gives R0_5>3, so the original
test (47) at the Silver level zeta=1 has value less than-1/D_5. Law C is
discarded at that gate. Its positive entire-plane compression (43) and
its actual full-space failure (49) demonstrate why paying N is insufficient.

## 8. Scope of the three failures and the next unpaid interface

All three specified laws have explicit final nonnegative finite/optimum
coefficients, full value flow and contact. Every original coordinate and
physical bridge is retained. All new bilinear directions and signed
decrements are displayed; positivity is rejected where the displayed
counterexample leaves an unpaid direction. None is presented as a full lift.

Law A: H2 and full Silver pass; two source restrictions are jointly injective
on the actual plane, but the sign-valid left circulation is a negative
square there. Reversing precisely that channel requires subtracting two
zero original multipliers. The exact original zero-boundary test (34) makes
the whole m-matrix negative at EVERY q. This is a scoped paired-row failure,
not a theorem that two sources can never work.

Law B: H2 mixed gates and full Silver pass, and the ENTIRE N is initially
positive at H4. The whole two-variable form becomes strictly negative by
H9 and stays negative at the actual m, at every q. This law has uniform
late normalized retention>1/2, so simply making the late retention product
positive cannot cure its earlier negative state.

Law C: H2 mixed gates and the ENTIRE N at H5 pass, yet the original mixed
test (47) fails, including the Silver gate. Its actual required full-word
inflation grows at least as(24/49)(m-4). It does not supply a useful bound
by resetting or hiding the child's mixed coefficient.

The missing mechanism remains explicit finite-row transport that pays BOTH
innovation degrees with allowed orientations and retains enough curvature
WITHOUT losing the original initial-point mixed terms or other gradients.
Two independent sources, a positive2-by2plane compression, and sustained
late inheritance are separately insufficient in these concrete families.
A new law must meet all these interfaces jointly. The failed equations
here do not exclude a different contextual completion or more varied rows.
No fourth law, unallocated repair, generic PEP/PSD reformulation, or numerical
surrogate is offered as progress toward the original target.

Accepted fact5fd396eba75651e7 gives ONLY
B_(2^m-1)/D_m<237/250 for the SAME enclosing-m tau1/4 physical word,
for every m>=1536. An ACTUAL full-function upper W<=zeta_m/D_m with
uniform sup zeta_m<250/237 would turn that scalar comparison into a strict
unbounded-horizon improvement. No such upper is supplied here. No restricted
witness-family optimum is reinterpreted as an upper for unrestricted W.
The original all-word exact-profile theorem remains OPEN.

## 9. Search, resource, honesty, and glossary record

Guidance93fb1d428186ede2 and TASK.md authorize ONE analytical invocation
and at mostTHREE explicit laws, with ZERO computation or formal gate. All
new mathematics above is ONE jointly UNVERIFIED draft. No intermediate
claim has been accepted or built on as a fact. Independent attack or formal
promotion would require the main's separately selected allocation.

Skills: query-memory, direct-proving, construct-counterexamples,
search-math-results and identify-key-failures. The search stage is
mature_subproblem; active program coupled finite-row innovation payment;
missing mechanism joint allowed orientation, original mixed curvature and
whole-word retention; mode repair; intent proof-method background.
One Matlas query returned short-step self-contraction (2003.04201),

Their step/algorithm hypotheses do not supply this long-step finite-row law.
One fallback web call with two queries recovered related composability and

These three mutations remain within the already audited original-row regime,
